```text
251 复合主键（`session_id, id`）是 v2 迁移引入的。原因很具体：主张编号每场会话都从 `C1` 重新开始，

254 ## 3.6 HTTP 层：一个端口，两种响应
255 
256 | 接口 | 用途 |
257 | --- | --- |
258 | `GET /api/health` | 模式、跑法、模型、卡带目录，以及可选运行模式清单与「联网是否配好」 |
259 | `GET /api/models` | 联网模式下可用的模型清单（配不全时**一个网络包都不发**，直接返回缺哪个环境变量） |
260 | `GET /api/demo` | 内置示例简历与 JD |
261 | `POST /api/import` | 上传 PDF / Word / 文本，解析成简历正文（字节不存留；413 太大 / 415 格式不支持 / 422 解析不出文字） |
262 | `GET /api/contracts?panel=` | 意图契约全文 + 指纹（纯确定性，不调模型、不读库） |
263 | `POST /api/sessions` | 建档（useDemo 或 resumeText，可指定 `mode` 与 `model`：模式与模型随会话落库） |
264 | `POST /api/sessions/:id/run` | 跑沙盘：默认 SSE 直播，`?stream=0` 走 JSON |
265 | `GET /api/sessions/:id/events?from=` | 事件续传 |
266 | `GET /api/sessions/:id/report.md` | 下载 Markdown 报告 |
267 | `GET /api/sessions/:id/report.html?print=1` | 可打印 HTML 报告（`print=1` 时自动调 `window.print()`，PDF 由浏览器另存） |
268 | `GET /api/sessions/:id/report.doc` | Word 报告（同一份 HTML + `application/msword`） |
269 | `GET /api/stats?session=` | 调用账本统计 |
270
```

```text
349 ## 3.8 本章要点
350 
351 - 服务端零构建是整条复现路径的地基，它成立的前提是被实测验证过的，不是查文档查来的。
352 - 模块按依赖方向切：shared 无 IO、server 不知前端、web 不引 shared（用测试守漂移）。
353 - LLM 适配层一个接口三种实现，卡带用提示词指纹寻址，缺卡带时响亮报错而不是静默降级。
354 - 直播与历史共用同一个折叠器，保证「刷新后看到的和刚才看到的是同一场」。
355 - 门禁四道，其中真进程冒烟是不可替代的那一道。
```

</details>

<details><summary>✅ 执行结果（原始 2175 字符，已折叠）</summary>

```text
306 
307 解析按格式分三条路，每条都尽量少欠依赖：PDF 用 `unpdf`（纯 JS，零原生编译），`.docx` 自己解 zip
308 （`node:zlib` 的 `inflateRawSync` 拆出 `word/document.xml`，不引 SDK），纯文本先按 UTF-8 解、出现替换字符再试 GBK
309 （中文简历的现实情况）。老版 `.doc` 是二进制复合文档，明确拒绝并提示另存为 `.docx`——**宁可给一句可执行的话，也不给一段看起来像正文的乱码**。
310 选型对比与三个反直觉的 PDF 行为见 ADR-0007、findings F39；导入器自己有 11 条单测，HTTP 分档另有 4 条。
311 
312 ### 3.6.5 运行模式与模型：逐场选，且随会话留档
313 
314 三种模式（离线回放 / 联网直连 / 联网录制）在**页面里逐场选择**，选择结果写进会话行：`llmMode` 落模式，`meta.model` 落模型名。跑的时候按会话行建客户端——单一事实来源在库里，不需要内存里的映射表，翻回一周前的会话也能说清它当时是怎么跑出来的。
315 
316 `GET /api/models` 只在显式选择联网后才被前端调用：先 `resolveLiveConfig`，缺 `LLM_BASE_URL` / `LLM_API_KEY` 就**一个网络包都不发**，直接把缺哪个变量告诉界面。拉不到清单不等于不能用——界面是 `input + datalist`，可以手填模型名；`/models` 返回什么也**不是可用性证据**（本机实测它只列出两款模型，而实际能调通四款）。见 D33、D34、F41。
317 ### 3.6.6 自定义面试官：一句话补成契约，没有 Key 也能配
318 
319 五位预设解决的是「计算机岗位」。可这个产品要能给任何行业用，就不能把面试官写死在代码里。
320 于是自建面试官的产物**不是一段自由文本，而是同一份 `IntentContract`**——它和预设共用渲染、共用指纹、共用约束条款，
321 所以第 2 章讲的「意图可解释、可版本化、可 diff」对自建面试官一样成立。
322 
323 链路是三段，每段都留了不联网的出口：
324 
325 1. **描述 → 草稿**（`POST /api/interviewers/draft`，不落库）。模型返回的 JSON 必须过 `interviewerDraftSchema`（名称 1–24 字、目标 8–200 字、手法 2–5 条…），
326    过不了就不收。用户勾「只用本地规则」时走 `localDraftFromText`：纯规则、确定性、不联网，靠一张措辞表把话翻成手法
327    （说
…（此处省略 975 字符，原始长度 2175）
```

```text
# ADR-0007：简历导入自己拆包，不引入会破坏「10 分钟复现」的解析栈

- 状态：已采纳
- 日期：2026-10-26
- 相关：ADR-0001（服务端零构建）、ADR-0004（离线回放）

## 背景

用户提出「支持 doc 和 pdf 导入简历」（m04531 第 2 项）。看着是一件小事，实际上它把
产品的入口从「你得会复制粘贴」变成「选一个文件」，而这一项的门槛决定了非计算机专业的
用户能不能用起来。难点全在**解析**：一份简历可能是 PDF（文字版或扫描件）、.docx、
老版 .doc、纯文本。

这里有一条不能破的约束：评委要在 10 分钟内把整条链路跑起来。任何需要本地编译的
原生依赖、或任何体积巨大且带可选原生模块的库，都会把这条承诺变成赌博。

## 决策

1. **PDF 用 unpdf。** 它是纯 JS、零依赖（内联了自己裁剪过的 pdf.js 构建），
   npm 安装即用，不需要任何本地编译。对比过的 pdfjs-dist 有 34.9 MB，

```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

第 4 项完成。现在剩第 8、7、10、11、12 项。

按顺序：
- 第 8 项：确认问题后自动改简历并导出 PDF/DOC
- 第 7 项：零门槛安装
- 第 10 项：桌面快捷方式（需用户同意）
- 第 11 项：可增加功能清单
- 第 12 项：全功能测试

先做第 8 项。这是最有价值的功能：用户确认一条建议后，自动把简历改掉，并能导出 PDF/DOC。

让我先看一下现有的 suggest 和建议数据结构，以及简历原文的锚点。

设计：
- 建议（Suggestion）已有：claimId、before、after、rationale、anchor（原文片段）
- 界面：每条建议有「采纳」按钮
- 采纳后：把简历原文里该片段替换成 after → 得到新的简历文本
- 累积多次采纳 → 简历修订版
- 导出：修订后的简历为 PDF / DOC

关键设计问题：
1. 替换必须精确：用锚点（start/end）而不是字符串查找，避免重复文本误替换。
2. 冲突处理：两条建议锚点重叠怎么办？拒绝第二条并说明。
3. 可撤销：采纳记录在状态里，能撤回。
4. 修订版简历可预览、可导出。

服务端：
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/edits` 记录采纳？还是纯前端状态？
  考虑到「可复现」和留档，应该落库。但简单起见：新增一张 `resume_edit` 表记录 session_id + suggestionId + 应用后的区间。
  
  更简单且更强的方案：**服务端计算修订文本**，因为服务端有原始的简历全文与锚点。
  - `POST /api/sessions/:id/revise` body: { accepted: string[] }（建议 id 列表）
  - 返回 { text, applied: [...], conflicts: [...] }
  - 纯函数，不落库；但界面需要能导出 → `GET /api/sessions/:id/revised.docx?accepted=...`

  hmm，导出需要带着 accepted 列表。用 query 传 id 列表可行（数量少）。

  或者：把采纳结果落库到 session 的 meta，这样导出 URL 干净。但「可复现」要求：同一场会话，采纳了哪些建议是一个状态。落库更符合项目的留档哲学。

  我倾向：新增 `resume_edit` 表（session_id, suggestion_id, applied_at），POST 采纳/取消采纳，GET 修订文本。导出 `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.docx`。

  但要注意：session 的简历原文在 session 表里（resume_text）。修订是基于原文 + 已采纳的建议集合重新计算的，所以是确定性的、可重放的。

4. 导出格式：
   - .docx：需要自己写 docx？没有 python-docx 在 node 里。可以手写一个最小 docx（zip + document.xml），用已有的 node:zlib deflateRawSync 写 zip。已经有 zip.ts 是解压用的（inflateRawSync），需要写压缩侧。
   - .doc（HTML + application/msword）：已有先例（报告 Word 走这条）。最简单、零依赖。
   - PDF：服务端不自造 PDF（ADR-0006），走可打印 HTML + 浏览器另存。

   所以：**修订版简历导出 .doc（HTML 同源）+ 可打印 HTML（另存 PDF）**，与报告导出同一套决策，不破 ADR-0006。

   但用户第 8 条明确说「提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出」——「或」！所以 .doc + 可打印 HTML 完全满足。

   不过手写 docx 其实也不难，而且 docx 比 .doc(HTML) 在 Word 里更正规。考虑到已有 zip 解压代码，写一个最小 docx 生成器（只含段落文本）大概 150 行。这样交付物更专业。

   我决定：**同时给 .docx（自己写最小 OOXML 打包）和可打印 HTML（另存 PDF）**。docx 用 deflateRawSync 写 zip。这样「PDF 或 DOC」两条都满足。

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now item 8: "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出".

Let me think about design. The suggestions already have before/after anchored to the resume. Requirement 8: after the user confirms a suggestion, automatically apply the modification to the resume, and provide PDF or DOC export.

So: a "修订稿" (revised resume) built by applying accepted suggestions to the original resume text (by anchor replacement), then export as .doc (Word HTML) and PDF (printable HTML).

Design:
- shared: `applySuggestions(resume, suggestions)` → { text, applied[], skipped[] } — apply by anchor: replace [start,end) with after, process from end to start to keep offsets valid. Skip overlapping suggestions. Deterministic.
- server: POST /api/sessions/:id/revise with { accepted: string[] (claimIds) } → returns revised text + stats. Or store the accepted set in session meta? Better: compute on the fly; export endpoints:
  - GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.docx? — actually .doc via HTML.
  - GET /api/sessions/:id/revise.md / revise.doc / revise.html?print=1
- Also need "确认" UI: in the suggestion cards, a checkbox "采纳这条修改", and a panel showing the revised resume with a diff, plus buttons 导出 Word / 导出 PDF / 复制全文.

Also should the revised resume be persisted? Keep it simple but reproducible: store accepted ids in session meta (`meta.accepted`), so export URL can be re-fetched. Actually simpler: pass accepted list as query param `?accept=C1,C3`. But long. Hmm.

Let me think about what's most defensible for the competition: "用户确认后自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出". A clean design:

1. shared/src/revise.ts: 
   - `ReviseInput { resumeText, suggestions: {claimId, before, after, anchor}[] , acceptedIds }`
   - `reviseResume(resumeText, suggestions, acceptedIds)` → `{ text, changes: {claimId, from, to, at:TextSpan}[], skipped: {claimId, reason}[] }`
   - Rules: only accepted; must have valid anchor within text and text.slice(start,end)===quote (or normalized); apply from the end so offsets stay valid; overlapping → skip the later one with reason "与前一条修改重叠".
   - Also `diffLines`? Maybe `summarizeChanges`.

2. server: 
   - `POST /api/sessions/:id/revise` body {accepted: string[]} → returns {text, changes, skipped, chars}. Persist accepted in session meta? I'd persist to make export links stable: saveSessionMeta. Hmm, but then POST is a mutation. That's fine — the resume text is already in the DB? Let's check: session has resume text stored. Let me check the store schema for session columns.

Let me look at the session row and assembleSession to know where resumeText lives.

Also for export: `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.doc` and `resume.html?print=1` and `resume.md`. Reuse report-html style.

Now let me plan item 8: "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出".

So: after verdicts/suggestions, the user confirms a suggestion, and the system applies the change to the resume text, then exports the revised resume as PDF or DOC.

Design: 
- New feature: "简历修订" (Resume revision). Suggestions have anchor + before + after. Applying a suggestion = replace the anchored span with `after` in the resume text. Multiple suggestions applied → produce revised resume text.
- Need to handle conflicts: two suggestions on overlapping anchors. Apply from the end backwards to keep offsets valid.
- Export: PDF or DOC. For resume export, we can't use server-side PDF (ADR-0006 says no). But for resume we could produce a printable HTML with ?print=1 same as report, and .doc via application/msword. That's consistent with ADR-0006. 

So: POST /api/sessions/:id/revision { claimIds: string[] } → returns revised resume text + list of applied changes + rejected (conflict/not-applicable).
GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1 and /resume.doc.

And frontend: a "修订" panel where you check which suggestions to apply, see the diff (before/after), preview the revised resume, and download PDF/DOC.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Let me now plan item 8: "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出".

This is a big feature: after suggestions are generated, the user can confirm ("accept") a suggestion, and the resume gets modified accordingly, then export as PDF/DOC.

Design:
1. Server: a "resume rewrite" module. Given resumeText + accepted suggestions (claimId -> suggestion with before/after anchored), apply the edits deterministically (anchor-based splice), producing a new resume text. Must be verifiable: each edit replaces the anchored original text with `after`, and we must detect overlaps/conflicts.
2. Export: PDF via HTML print (same as report-html, ADR-0006 says server doesn't generate PDF itself — browser saves as PDF). But user explicitly asks "提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出". For resume: generate a clean HTML resume that prints to PDF, and a .doc (HTML + application/msword) like the report. That's consistent with ADR-0006.

Requirement 8: 提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出。

Design:
- New server module: `packages/server/src/rewrite.ts` (简历改写) — apply suggestions to resume text.
- Anchor-based replacement: suggestions have `before` and `anchor` (Suggestion.anchor). But we need to map to the resume text. The suggestion's anchor gives start/end in the resume text of the before fragment. Multiple adoptions need offset handling.
- New table? Better: store adopted rewrites in meta of the session? But must be durable and undoable. Let's add a `rewrite` table (schema v5) with (session_id, claim_id) primary key, before_text, after_text, start, end, created_at. Actually to make it robust: store the accepted list and recompute the new resume text from the original resume + accepted edits sorted by start descending.

Actually, simpler and more robust: store each accepted edit as a row: `rewrite(session_id, claim_id, start, end, before, after, created_at, updated_at)` with PRIMARY KEY (session_id, claim_id). Then `buildResume(session, edits)` = apply edits sorted by start desc to resumeText, verifying that resumeText.slice(start,end) === before each time; if mismatch, report conflict.

Then new endpoints:
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/rewrites` body {claimId, accepted?} or {claimId} to accept; DELETE to revoke.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume` → {text, edits, conflicts}
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1` and `.doc` — new resume document (clean resume, not the report).

The resume doc should be a *resume* rendering: just the rewritten text, nicely formatted? A resume text is plain text with sections. Let's render it as a document with the text preserved (pre-wrap), header with 姓名? We don't parse name. Simplest honest approach: render the rewritten resume text in a print-friendly page with a small footer noting it was revised via CrossExam and which claims were changed. Keep it modest.

```text
1 /**
```

```text
4  * 三条刻意的取舍：
5  *   1. 跑沙盘是 POST + SSE 而不是 WebSocket：单向推送用不上双向，
6  *      而且 SSE 自带 last-event-id 语义，重连按 seq 续传是协议里写好的。

8  *      它只负责把事件搬给前端，把结果交给报告渲染。
9  *   3. 每个事件都先落库再推送：前端崩了、刷新了，过程也还在，
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I have a good picture. Let me plan item 8: "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出".

Design:
1. Server: new module `packages/server/src/revise.ts` — apply accepted suggestions to resume text.
   - Input: resumeText + list of suggestion claimIds (accepted).
   - For each accepted suggestion, apply `after` replacing `before` at the anchor location.
   - Handle offset drift: apply from the end backwards (sort by anchor.start desc), so earlier offsets stay valid.
   - Verify anchor still grounded in the resume text; if not, report conflict.
   - Output: new resume text + applied records + skipped records.
2. Store: new table `revision` (session_id, id, claim_id, before, after, start, end, created_at) — or simpler: store as meta? Better a real table so it's queryable and revocable. Schema version 5.
   - Actually simpler and honest: store revisions in a table `revision(session_id TEXT, id TEXT, claim_id TEXT, start INTEGER, end INTEGER, before TEXT, after TEXT, created_at TEXT, PRIMARY KEY(session_id,id))`. Plus applied order.
   - Undo = delete one revision row.
   - But the resume text must be recomputed from base resume + remaining revisions each time. That's actually the cleanest: base resumeText in session row is never modified; revised text = apply all revisions in order. This makes undo trivially correct and the original always intact.
   - Offset drift handled by re-anchoring each revision's `before` against the current text (locateQuote), so we don't even need to trust stored start/end. But we do store start/end for reference.
   
   Careful: applying multiple revisions with re-anchoring each time. If a later revision's `before` no longer exists because an earlier revision changed that exact text, we report it as skipped/conflict. That's honest.

3. HTTP endpoints:
   - `POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions` body {claimIds: string[]} → apply accepted suggestions, returns {text, applied, skipped}
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/revisions` → list
   - `DELETE /api/sessions/:id/revisions/:revId` → undo
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume` → {original, revised, revisions}
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1` → revised resume as printable HTML (for PDF)
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.doc` → Word

4. New renderer `packages/server/src/resume-html.ts` — render revised resume as a clean one-page-ish HTML resume with print CSS, plus optionally a diff/appendix listing what changed. Actually user wants "把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出" — so the export should be the *revised resume*, a real resume document. Keep it clean: original formatting is lost since we only have plain text. <details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I have a clear picture. Let me plan item 8: "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出".

Design:
1. New module `packages/server/src/revise.ts` (or `packages/shared/src/revise.ts` for the pure application logic): apply accepted suggestions to resume text.
   - Key challenge: multiple acceptances with shifting offsets. Solution: apply from the end backwards (sort by anchor.start descending), so earlier offsets remain valid. Also validate each anchor is grounded before applying.
   - Also need overlap detection: two suggestions may target overlapping ranges. Reject overlapping (or apply the first one only).
   
2. Store: new table `revision` (session_id, id, claim_id, before, after, start, end, applied_at) with SCHEMA_VERSION = 5. Or simpler: store in meta? No — better a real table for queryability, and events.

3. HTTP endpoints:
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume` → current revised resume text + applied revisions list + pending suggestions with anchors.
   - `POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions` body {claimId} → apply that suggestion, return new text + revision record.
   - `DELETE /api/sessions/:id/revisions/:revId` → undo (recompute from scratch by re-applying remaining revisions).
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1` / `.doc` → render the revised resume as printable HTML.

Actually for undo, the cleanest design: store the base resume text (session.resumeText is the original, immutable) + list of applied revisions (each with claimId, before, after, anchor start/end at the time of application). To reconstruct the current text: re-apply in order from the original text... but offsets shift. Better: store revisions as ordered list, and re-apply by anchoring the `before` text in the *current* text at each step. Since `before` is a verbatim slice of the original resume, after previous revisions it should still be present (unless overlapping). So reconstruction = fold over revisions in application order, each time `locateQuote(current, before)` then replace. That's deterministic and idempotent, and undo = drop that revision and re-fold.

This is elegant and avoids storing offsets at all. And it also handles the overlap case: if a later revision's `before` no longer locates (because an earlier one replaced that region), we mark it as failed.

So the revision table stores: session_id, seq, claim_id, before, after, created_at. Reconstruction is a pure fold.

4. Export: `resume-html.ts` — renders a clean resume document (not the report). Reuse the "printable HTML + .doc" pattern. Should be a proper resume layout: sections from `segmentResume`, or simply render the text with paragraph structure. Since the resume is plain text, a simple, dignified layout: title (job title), then paragraphs preserving line structure, headings bold when they're section headers (using segmentResume). Also optionally include an appendix listing what changed (采纳了哪几条建议) — good for the competition demo: "改了什么" traceable. But the exported resume itself should be clean; add an optional `?notes=1` appendix.

5. Frontend: in ClaimBoard's suggestion block, add "采纳这条" button → POST revision → update state. Need a new pane/section showing the current revised resume text with an export bar (PDF / Word / 复制). Maybe add to the 简历原文 pane: a toggle between「原稿」and「修改稿」, plus buttons.

Design:

- New table? Or store in meta? Better: new table `revision` (session_id, claim_id, accepted_at, before, after, start, end) — actually simpler: store accepted claim ids in session meta? But AGENTS says schema migrations per version. Let me think about minimal but correct.

But revisions are user actions that accumulate; meta JSON update via saveSessionMeta. That's acceptable and avoids migration. However the meta could grow. Each revision is small (before/after text). Actually, a cleaner approach consistent with existing design: event table is append-only source of truth; but revisions happen after the run. Hmm, adding an event type `revision` would require state machine changes in web state.ts too, plus replay. That's actually elegant: the revision is an event, replayable. But the event table is for the run... appendEvent exists in repo. Events have seq per session. Replaying revision events in state would need a new event type in RunEvent union in web api.ts. That's doable but heavier.

Simpler and still honest: store in meta.revisions (array), and expose via GET /api/sessions/:id/resume (returns {text, revisions, applied: [...], skipped: [...]}). And POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions {claimId} to accept / DELETE to undo.

Let me think about what's the minimal coherent feature set for the user requirement:
- Frontend: each suggestion card gets "采纳这条改写" button (data-shot="accept-<claimId>") and after accepting shows "已采纳" + "撤销".
- A new pane or section: "改后的简历" with export buttons: 导出 PDF / 导出 Word / 复制文本.
- Server: 
  - `POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions` body {claimId} → validates session exists, suggestion exists with status suggested, applies → saves into meta.revisions → returns {revisions, resume: revisedText, problems}
  - `DELETE /api/sessions/:id/revisions/:claimId` → undo
  - `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume` → { original, revised, revisions: [{claimId, before, after, acceptedAt, anchor}], applied/skipped }
  - `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1` and `.doc` → rendered resume document (not the report; a resume). Reuse print approach: HTML styled like a resume (A4, clean), print → PDF, .doc for Word.

3. Offset handling: applying multiple revisions — anchor positions refer to the original text. Apply in descending start order so earlier offsets stay valid. Also detect overlaps: two accepted revisions whose anchors overlap → reject the second (or apply first-wins). And `before` must still match `sliceAnchor` on original text.

Also important: after applying, the resulting text must still be valid; verify each replacement's `before` equals text.slice(start,end).

Now I have a good picture. Let me design item 8.

Requirement 8: "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出"

Design:
- After a session completes, for each suggestion (before/after), the user can accept (采纳) it. Accepted suggestions get applied to the resume text (replacing before with after at the anchor range), producing a new resume text ("修订版简历").
- Multiple accepts → need offset handling. Best approach: server stores a list of accepted suggestion ids (claimIds) per session in meta, and the revised resume is **recomputed deterministically** from the original resume text + the accepted suggestion list each time. That avoids offset drift entirely and is fully reversible (un-accept = recompute). This is a strong design decision: don't mutate the text incrementally, recompute from base + set of applied edits.
- Apply order: sort by anchor.start descending, then splice. Overlapping edits must be rejected (can't apply two overlapping edits).
- Export: HTML (print → PDF) and .doc, same route as report. Reuse a renderer: `renderResumeHtml(revised, {original, applied})`.

New API:
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/revision` → { applied: string[], base: resumeText, revised: resumeText, edits: [{claimId, anchor, before, after, applied}], conflicts: [...], chars }
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/revision` with body {claimId, applied: boolean} → toggles one, returns the same view.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1` → revised resume HTML
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.doc`

Also need shared pure function for applying edits — put in shared so web can preview? Web needs to show the diff/preview. But web doesn't import shared package (decision D11: web imports only @crossexam/shared/domain.ts for labels). Hmm — actually decision D11 says web doesn't bring in the shared package because of node:crypto. So the apply logic should live server-side, and web gets the revised text via API. That's simpler and consistent.

Actually, where to put the apply function? It's a pure text function. Options: shared (server-only import) or server. Since only server needs it, put it in `packages/server/src/revision.ts`. But a pure function that tests can hit... server tests are fine.

Hmm, but actually there's a case for shared: the "apply edits" logic is domain logic. But web can't import it. Keep in server: `packages/server/src/revision.ts`.

Let me define:

```ts
export interface RevisionEdit {
  claimId: string;
  anchor: Anchor;
  before: string;
  after: string;
  applied: boolean;
  /** 无法应用的原因（重叠 / 锚点已失效） */
  blocked: string | null;
}

export interface RevisionView {
  sessionId: string;
  base: string;       // 原简历
  revised: string;    // 应用了已采纳编辑之后的简历
  edits: RevisionEdit[];
  appliedCount: number;
  chars: number;
  notes: string[];
}
```

Core function: `applyEdits(base: string, edits: {anchor, after}[]): { text: string; applied: string[]; blocked: ... }`.

Rules:
1. Anchor must still be grounded: `isGrounded(base, anchor)` — source.slice(start,end) === quote. If not, blocked = '锚点已经对不上原简历（简历被改过？）'.
2. Overlap: sort by start; if a.start < prev.end → blocked = '与另一条修改区间重叠'.
3. Apply descending by start so offsets don't shift.

Also verify `before` matches the anchored slice: the suggestion's before should equal anchor.quote (or at least be located). Actually in suggest.ts, before must resolveAnchor successfully. Let me check whether anchor.quote === before. Let me read the rest of suggest.ts to see how the Suggestion anchor is built.

Now the resume export renderer: `packages/server/src/resume-html.ts` — render revised resume as a clean printable resume (not the report). Reuse STYLE-ish but distinct: a resume document. Should show: title (job title), the revised text in paragraphs, and a footer listing applied changes? Maybe a second section "本次改动清单" appended (before → after) so the user can see what changed — but for a resume PDF you want a clean resume. Solution: two outputs? Simpler: one HTML with a checkbox-ish query param `?diff=1` that appends the change list. Careful: the revised text is base with edits applied; segmenting the revised text gives segments with new offsets. Since edits may add/remove characters, segment on revised text directly. Yes.

Since suggestions were generated against the session's resumeText, and applying multiple edits shifts offsets, need to apply in descending order of start, and detect overlaps/conflicts.
  - Validate: `isGrounded(resumeText, suggestion.anchor)` → slice equals quote. Also re-run `newNumbersIn(after, resumeText)` gate — the "only change wording, not facts" rule must hold at apply time too (defense in depth).

Plan:

Needs to handle multiple suggestions with offset shifting: sort by anchor.start, apply from end to start, or apply sequentially with recompute. Must verify each suggestion's `before` still anchors in the *current* text.

2. Store: new table `resume_edit` (session_id, claim_id, applied_at, before, after, start, end, ordinal) with composite key, or store in meta? Better: a table `edit` for persisted accepted suggestions. Schema version 5.

3. HTTP: 
   - `POST /api/sessions/:id/edits` (accept a suggestion) — or simpler: `PUT /api/sessions/:id/resume-edits` with the list of accepted claim ids. Let's design: 
     - `GET /api/sessions/:id/rewrite` → returns { accepted: [...], resumeText, applied: [...], rejected: [...], changes }
     - `POST /api/sessions/:id/rewrite` body { claimIds: string[] } → applies, persists, returns new text + per-item result.
     - `GET /api/sessions/:id/rewrite.html?print=1` → renders the rewritten resume as a printable HTML (for PDF) 
     - `GET /api/sessions/:id/rewrite.doc` → .doc export.
   - Also should support downloading the rewritten resume as .txt? PDF/DOC is what's requested.

4. Web: In ClaimBoard, each suggestion block gets a checkbox/button「采纳这条改写」with data-shot. App state keeps a Set of accepted claim ids; a panel/toolbar shows「改写后的简历」with export buttons (PDF/DOC/txt). Probably a new component `RewritePanel.tsx` or integrate into ResumePane as a toggle between 原文 / 改写后.

Given the effort, let me design carefully but keep it tractable:

Server-side rewrite logic (`packages/server/src/rewrite.ts`):
```ts
export interface RewriteEdit { claimId: string; start: number; end: number; before: string; after: string; }
export interface RewriteSkip { claimId: string; reason: string; }
export interface RewriteResult { text: string; edits: RewriteEdit[]; skipped: RewriteSkip[]; }
export function applySuggestions(resumeText: string, suggestions: readonly {claimId, suggestion: Suggestion|null}[]): RewriteResult
```
Algorithm: collect candidate edits (only status suggested, suggestion non-null), for each verify `resolveAnchor(resumeText, {quote: before})` — actually use the stored anchor and check `isGrounded`. Better to re-locate via resolveAnchor on `before` so that anchor staleness doesn't matter. Then detect overlaps → skip the later one with a reason. Then apply descending by start.

Also gate: after replacement text must not introduce new numbers — already guaranteed by suggest.ts gate, but re-check at apply time (cheap, honest).

5. Resume HTML rendering: `packages/server/src/resume-html.ts` — render the rewritten resume text as a clean A4 resume HTML with print support, and .doc export (same HTML + application/msword). Keep it simple: parse segments via segmentResume, render headers as h2, bullets as li, paragraphs as p.

Also should show which lines were changed? A "修改说明" section listing accepted edits — good for the report. Let's include an appendix optionally.

6. Persistence: store accepted edits in a new table so history reload shows them. Schema v5: table `resume_edit(session_id, claim_id, ordinal, start, end, before_text, after_text, applied_at)` PK (session_id, claim_id). Repo: `saveEdits(db, sessionId, edits)`, `listEdits(db, sessionId)`, `deleteEdit` maybe. Simpler: `replaceEdits(db, sessionId, edits)` in one transaction.

Actually, storing the full rewrite text? The session table has resume_text (original). Rewritten text is derivable from original + edits. Store edits only — that's the honest, diff-based approach, and it makes the "可撤销" trivially true.

7. API shape:
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/rewrite` → `{ sessionId, original, text, edits: [{claimId, before, after, start, end}], skipped: [...], accepted: [claimIds] }`
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/rewrite` body `{ claimIds: string[] }` → applies & persists, returns same view. Validate claimIds exist & have suggestions; unknown → 400.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/rewrite.html?print=1` → printable resume
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/rewrite.doc` → Word

8. Web:
- api.ts: `fetchRewrite(sessionId)`, `saveRewrite(sessionId, claimIds)`, `rewriteHtmlUrl`, `rewriteDocUrl`, types `RewriteView`.
- state: keep accepted claim ids in App state (not reducer) — `accepted: string[]`.

  - `POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions` {claimId, action: 'apply'|'revert'} or {claimIds: [...]} → apply/revert

- Key technical problem: applying replacements with anchor offsets that shift. Design:

Server side:

Approach: compute edits from the ORIGINAL text, sort descending by start, then apply. Each edit = {claimId, start, end, before, after}. Validate: no overlapping ranges (if overlap → reject that one). Then apply in descending order.
   - Output: { text, applied: [{claimId, before, after, start, end}], skipped: [{claimId, reason}] }.

```ts
export function applyEdits(resumeText: string, edits: readonly EditRequest[]): RewriteResult
```

Now let me plan item 8 implementation concretely.

Design for 第 8 项「确认问题后自动改简历，并导出 PDF/DOC」:

Core needs:
1. A resume-revision engine in shared or server: apply accepted suggestions to resume text by anchor ranges, descending by start.
2. Persist accepted edits per session so it's revocable and reproducible.
3. Export the revised resume as PDF / DOC (reuse the HTML print route; new endpoint /api/sessions/:id/resume.pdf? Actually the existing pattern: report.html?print=1 opens print dialog → user saves PDF. Same for resume: /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1 and /resume.doc).

Let me design:

**shared/src/revision.ts** (pure, testable):
- `ResumeEdit { claimId, anchor: Anchor, before, after, acceptedAt? }` — actually reuse Suggestion.
- `applyEdits(resumeText, edits): {text, applied, skipped}` — sort by anchor.start desc, skip overlaps, skip when text.slice(start,end) !== before (quote mismatch → the resume changed), return offsets.
- `revisionDigest`? maybe not needed.
- `diffLines`? Maybe a small diff for display. Keep it simple: show before/after per edit.

**store**: new table `resume_edit` (v5): session_id, claim_id, start, end, before, after, created_at. Primary key (session_id, claim_id). Repo: `saveResumeEdit`, `listResumeEdits`, `deleteResumeEdit`.

Actually simpler and more robust: store accepted edits in a table so re-applying is deterministic and undo = delete row.

**HTTP**:
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/edits` body `{claimId}` → accept suggestion for that claim: read session view, find suggestion, apply to a *fresh* application of all accepted edits in order, validate, persist, return `{ok, edit, revision:{text, chars, applied, skipped}}`. Also `DELETE /api/sessions/:id/edits/:claimId` → undo.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/revision` → `{edits, text, base, applied, skipped}`.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1`, `/resume.doc` — rendered revised resume.

Better: keep application logic in a module `packages/server/src/revision.ts` that takes resumeText + suggestions + accepted claimIds and produces the revised text.

**web**:
- ClaimBoard suggestion block gets 「采纳并改简历」/「已采纳」 toggle button (`data-shot="accept-<claimId>"`), calls API, App keeps `revision` state, and a new pane or the header shows 「简历修订版（N 处）」+ 导出 PDF / Word.
- ResumePane could show revised text — better: add a toggle in ResumePane header「原文 / 修订版」.

- shared/src/revision.ts with `applySuggestions(resumeText, edits)`.
- server: store v5 table, repo functions, `packages/server/src/revision.ts` orchestrating, HTTP endpoints, resume HTML rendering (reuse STYLE from report-html — need to export STYLE or a shared `pageHtml` helper).
- web: accept buttons + revision state + export links + ResumePane toggle.
- tests: shared revision tests, server http tests, web tests for api/revision state.
- e2e + shots + docs.

Item 8: "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出"

Design:
1. shared: new module `revision.ts` — apply accepted suggestions to resume text.
   - `RevisionInput`: list of {id (claimId), before, after, anchor}
   - `applyRevisions(text, revisions)` → apply sorted by start descending; each revision must verify that text.slice(start,end) matches... Actually after applying one revision, earlier (lower start) offsets unchanged since we go descending. But need to check for overlaps → reject overlapping.
   - Return `{text, applied: [...], skipped: [...]}`.
   - Also produce a diff-ish list for display.

2. server store: new table `revision` (v5) with session_id, claim_id, before_text, after_text, start, end, status, created_at. Or simpler: store accepted revisions in session meta? Better a table for auditability. SCHEMA_VERSION = 5.

Actually, to keep it lean but still honest: a table `revision` with PRIMARY KEY (session_id, claim_id), columns: seq/ordinal, quote/before/after/start/end/exact, created_at, reverted_at? For undo, simplest: DELETE row (撤销). And a "resume_text" revision output is computed on the fly from the original resume text + applied revisions.

3. server: `packages/server/src/revision.ts`? Actually shared is better since front-end also needs to preview. Let's put `applyRevisions` in shared (`packages/shared/src/revision.ts`) and export from index.

4. HTTP endpoints:
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/revisions` → list applied revisions + current revised text
   - `POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions` body {claimId} or {claimIds} → accept suggestion(s)
   - `DELETE /api/sessions/:id/revisions/:claimId` → undo
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1` and `/resume.doc` → export revised resume
   - Maybe also `/resume.txt` and `/resume.md`.

5. web: ClaimBoard suggestion block gets 「采纳这条修改」 button; a new panel/pane showing 修订版简历 with export buttons (PDF/Word/txt). Undo button per applied revision.

Given time, I'll implement:

**shared/src/revision.ts**
```ts
export interface RevisionInput { claimId: string; before: string; after: string; anchor: TextSpan }
export interface AppliedRevision { claimId: string; before: string; after: string; start: number; end: number; }
export interface SkippedRevision { claimId: string; reason: string }
export interface RevisionResult { text: string; applied: AppliedRevision[]; skipped: SkippedRevision[]; unchanged: boolean }
export function applyRevisions(source: string, revisions: readonly RevisionInput[]): RevisionResult
```
Rules:
- sort by anchor.start descending
- skip if start<0/end>len/end<=start
- skip if source.slice(start,end) !== before (allow normalized? better: use resolveAnchor to re-locate before; if fails skip with reason). Actually stronger: verify `source.slice(start,end) === before`; if not, try `resolveAnchor(source,{quote:before})`; if found use that span; else skip "原文对不上".
- skip overlaps with already-applied span (since descending, keep a `lowest` boundary; if rev.end > lowest → overlap → skip).
- skip if after===before.

**server store v5**: revision table.
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS revision (
  session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES session(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  claim_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  before_text TEXT NOT NULL,
  after_text TEXT NOT NULL,
  start INTEGER NOT NULL,
  end INTEGER NOT NULL,
  exact INTEGER NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (session_id, claim_id)
)
```
repo: `saveRevision`, `listRevisions`, `deleteRevision`, `deleteRevisions`.

**server/src/revision-doc.ts** — render revised resume as printable HTML, reuse STYLE? STYLE is not exported. I'll export STYLE and esc from report-html, or write a separate module with its own style. Cleaner: export `STYLE` and `esc` from report-html and reuse in a new `resume-html.ts`.

Render: header「修订版简历」, a list of applied changes (before → after), then the resume text in `<pre>`-ish paragraphs preserving line breaks. Keep line breaks: split by \n and emit <p>.

- `GET /api/sessions/:id/revisions` → {revisions, revisedText, changed}
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions` body {claimIds?: string[]} or {claimId} → accepts suggestions that exist and are 'suggested'; returns updated state
- `DELETE /api/sessions/:id/revisions/:claimId`
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1`, `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.doc`

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I have a good picture. Let me design item 8 (采纳建议 → 改写简历 → 导出 PDF/DOC).

Design decisions:

1. **Where does the revised resume live?** Options:
   - (a) Client-side only: apply edits in the browser, render a printable HTML via a new window. But then no persistence, no server-side export of "revised resume".
   - (b) Server-side: store accepted suggestions as revisions (a new table `revision`), compute revised text server-side deterministically, and expose `/api/sessions/:id/resume.html`, `.doc`, `.txt` endpoints.

   (b) is much better for the competition: it's an evidence chain (accepted revisions are recorded, reversible, replayable). And it reuses report-html's STYLE approach.

2. **Deterministic application**: accept list of suggestion claimIds → apply by anchor start descending. Need to guard: overlapping spans (can't apply two overlapping edits). Since claims are anchored to distinct quotes, overlap is unlikely but must be checked.

3. **Data model** (schema v5): new table `revision`:
```
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS revision (
  session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES session(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  claim_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL,
  before TEXT NOT NULL,
  after TEXT NOT NULL,
  start INTEGER NOT NULL,
  end INTEGER NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (session_id, claim_id)
)
```
Actually primary key on (session_id, claim_id) means one accepted revision per claim — that's right: 一条主张最多采纳一次改写。Re-accepting updates it. Un-accepting deletes the row. Good and simple.

Hmm, but should we allow multiple revisions of the same claim (adopt, then adopt again)? The suggestion is fixed per claim, so one row is fine.

4. **Revised text computation**: `applyRevisions(resumeText, revisions)` — sort by start desc, splice. Also export a function to compute which spans conflict.

Important subtlety: the anchor start/end were computed against the original resume text. After applying one edit, offsets shift for everything after it. Applying in descending start order solves that. Good.

5. **HTTP API**:
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume` → `{ text, original, revisions: [{claimId, before, after, start, end}], conflicts: [] }`? Maybe simpler: `GET /api/sessions/:id/revisions` returns list + revised text.
   - `POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions` `{ claimId, accepted: true }` → save; `{ claimId, accepted: false }` → delete. Or use `PUT`/`DELETE /api/sessions/:id/revisions/:claimId`. RESTful is cleaner: `PUT /api/sessions/:id/revisions/:claimId` (adopt), `DELETE .../:claimId` (undo).
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1` / `.doc` / `.txt` → revised resume as printable doc.
   - Maybe also `POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions` batch accept all — "一键采纳全部" is a nice UX. Let's support `{ claimIds: string[] }` PUT body batch. Hmm, keep it simple: PUT with optional body `{claimIds}`? Let me do:
     - `PUT /api/sessions/:id/revisions` body `{ claimIds: string[] }` → replace the whole accepted set atomically (idempotent, handles accept-all and undo-all with the same call). Then `DELETE /api/sessions/:id/revisions` → clear all.
     
     Actually "replace whole set" is the cleanest model: the client sends the set of claim ids it has accepted; server validates each has a valid suggestion; computes revised text; returns `{ revisions, revisedText, conflicts }`. Undo = send set without that id. Accept-all = send all suggestible claim ids.

     That's nice and idempotent. But is it good UX-wise for concurrency? Single user, fine.

     I'll go with `PUT /api/sessions/:id/revisions` body `{ claimIds: string[] }`, and `DELETE` for clear-all. Response includes the full state so the client doesn't need a second GET.

6. **Conflict handling**: two accepted suggestions whose anchors overlap → cannot both be applied. Return 400 with a clear Chinese message naming the two claims. Also a suggestion whose `before` no longer matches the resume text at that span (shouldn't happen, but check `resumeText.slice(start,end) === before` — actually anchor.quote may differ in punctuation from before if normalized. Better: verify `resolveAnchor(resumeText, {quote: before})` still succeeds and its span equals the stored anchor span. If not, 409.

7. **Revised resume rendering**: new file `packages/server/src/resume-doc.ts`:
   - `renderResumeHtml(input: {title, text, revisions, meta})` — printable A4 HTML with the revised text as paragraphs (segment-based), with changed spans highlighted (mark with 采纳 badge), plus a footer listing which claims changed. Reuse `STYLE`-like CSS but its own (resume style should look like a resume, not a report).
   - `resumeHtmlFileName` / `resumeDocFileName`.
   
   Highlighting: to show what changed, render the revised text with the after-spans marked. Since after-spans positions differ, compute them by applying edits and tracking positions.

8. **Frontend**: in ClaimBoard's suggestion block, add「采纳这条改写」button (data-shot `accept-<claimId>`) that toggles. Plus a summary bar in ResumePane or a new pane? Simplest: put the adopt controls in ClaimBoard, and add an "改写后的简历" panel accessible from the header: buttons「导出简历 PDF」「导出简历 Word」「导出 .txt」 + 「清空全部采纳」. And show the revised text somewhere.

   Where to show revised resume? Best: ResumePane gets a toggle「原文 / 改写后」. That's a nice, minimal change: ResumePane already has actions slot? Let me check ResumePane — it takes text/focus/hits/title, no actions. I can add `actions?: ReactNode` and a `revised?: string` + `mode` toggle. Hmm, but ResumePane renders marks from anchors; for revised text, highlighting changed spans would need spans. I can pass `hits` = changed spans of revised text.

   Simpler and more honest: ResumePane shows the revised text when toggle is on, and I pass the revised change spans as `hits`. The `focus` is still an original-text anchor which would be wrong in revised mode — so in revised mode, clicking a quote should switch back to original mode. Handle: `onFocus` in revised mode → set mode to original then focus. Actually the Quote component calls onFocus with anchor; App's dispatch focus. I can make the ResumePane, when in revised mode, ignore focus. Simplest: when user clicks a quote while in revised mode, auto-switch to original view. That's friendly.

   Hmm, complexity. Alternative: keep ResumePane as-is (original), and put the revised resume in a **modal overlay** "改写后的简历" with export buttons — similar to InterviewerStudio. That avoids messing with focus semantics, and gives a clean place for the diff list + export buttons. I like this better: `ResumeExport.tsx` overlay, opened by a header button「改写后的简历」which appears when at least one revision accepted... but users need to find it. Better: always show the button in the header next to reports, disabled/hidden until there's ≥1 accepted.

   Actually let me reconsider — the user's requirement #8: "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出". So: in each claim card's suggestion block there's an accept button; after accepting, a prominent export entry appears. A dedicated panel/overlay showing the revised resume with changed parts highlighted + 导出 PDF/Word/txt is exactly right.

   I'll build `packages/web/src/components/ResumeExport.tsx` overlay:
   - Header: 改写后的简历
   - Summary: 采纳了 N 条改写 · 原 N 字 → 现 M 字
   - Body: revised text rendered with changed spans marked
   - List of accepted revisions with undo buttons
   - Actions: 导出 PDF（打开 ?print=1）/ 导出 Word（.doc）/ 导出 .txt / 清空全部
   - data-shot: `resume-export`, `resume-export-close`, `resume-export-text`, `resume-export-pdf`, `resume-export-doc`, `resume-export-txt`, `resume-export-clear`, `resume-undo-<claimId>`

   And in ClaimBoard suggestion block: button 「采纳这条改写」(data-shot `accept-<claimId>`) / 「已采纳，撤销」(data-shot `undo-<claimId>`).

   State: add `revisions` to SandboxState? The revision set is server-side; on load of a session we should fetch it. Simplest: App holds `revisions: RevisionState | null` fetched when session loads/completes, and updated on PUT. ClaimBoard needs `accepted: (claimId) => boolean` + `onAccept/onUndo` callbacks.

9. **Tests**:
   - server: `packages/server/test/resume-revision.test.ts` — pure function tests for applyRevisions (descending order, overlap rejection, mismatch rejection).
   - server http tests: PUT/DELETE revisions, export endpoints, conflict 400, unknown claim 404/400.
   - web: maybe a small test for a helper that renders revised spans? Could add `packages/web/test/revision.test.ts` for the client-side span math (marking changed spans). Actually the server computes changed spans and returns them; web just renders. Keep web test minimal — maybe skip, or test that ClaimBoard... no DOM testing setup. I'll add a small pure helper `revisedSpans` on server side and test there.

10. **Docs**: ADR-0009 (采纳改写是可撤销的、确定性的、可导出的), D38/D39, findings, architecture 3.6.7, README, tech doc rebuild, screenshots (17-resume-export.png), e2e scenario.

That's a lot. Let me get moving. Order:
1. shared: `applyEdits` helper? Put revision logic in shared? The revision application is domain logic used by server only (and maybe web for preview). Put it in `packages/shared/src/revision.ts` so both can use — but web can't import shared (node:crypto). So server-only: `packages/server/src/revision.ts`. Fine.

Let me write `packages/server/src/revision.ts`:

```ts
import type { Suggestion } from '@crossexam/shared';
import type { RevisionRow } from './store/index.ts';

export interface RevisionCandidate {
  claimId: string;
  suggestion: Suggestion;
}

export interface RevisionPlan {
  revisions: RevisionRow[];      // 按 start 升序
  revisedText: string;
  spans: TextSpan[];             // 改写后文本里被替换的区间（用于高亮）
  conflicts: string[];           // 人类可读的冲突说明
}

export function planRevisions(resumeText: string, candidates: RevisionCandidate[], wanted: readonly string[]): RevisionPlan
```

Behavior:
- For each wanted claimId: find candidate. Missing → conflict "主张 X 没有可采纳的改写建议".
- Verify `resumeText.slice(anchor.start, anchor.end) === suggestion.before` — if not, conflict "改写建议的原文区间与简历原文不一致".
  Careful: anchor.quote is the exact source quote, and suggestion.before is what the model says the current text is; suggest.ts anchored `before` so anchor.quote should equal before (exact) or differ by normalization. Hmm! `resolveAnchor` may return an anchor whose `quote` is the source slice and `exact=false`. So `before` might differ from source slice (e.g. full-width punctuation). The replacement should replace the **source span** (anchor.start..end) with `after`. That's what suggest.ts intends: anchor is the located span of `before` in the resume.

  So verification: `resumeText.slice(start,end)` should be "equivalent" to before. Use `locateQuote(resumeText, before)`? Better: just re-run `resolveAnchor(resumeText, {quote: before})` and require the span to equal the stored anchor span. If equal → good.
- Overlap check between accepted spans: if two spans overlap → conflict.
- Sort by start ascending for display, apply descending.
- Compute revised spans: iterate ascending, track delta.

Let me now also handle "before 与原文不一致"（用户改过简历？不可能，同一场会话）— it's a safety net.

Now the store: schema v5 with `revision` table. Actually — do I need a table at all? I could store accepted revisions in `session.meta`. But AGENTS.md says architecture: a table is cleaner and queryable, and demonstrates schema migration maturity (v4→v5). But more migrations = more risk. Meta JSON is already used for panel/contracts. Hmm. Evidence-wise, a table is better: "采纳记录落库且可撤销" with an ordinal.

I'll add the table (v5). Migration is the cheapest kind (add table only), same as v4.

RevisionRow:
```ts
export interface RevisionRow {
  claimId: string;
  before: string;
  after: string;
  start: number;
  end: number;
  createdAt: string;
}
```
repo functions: `replaceRevisions(db, sessionId, rows, at?)` (DELETE all + INSERT batch in transaction), `listRevisions(db, sessionId)`, `deleteRevisions(db, sessionId)`.

HTTP:

Reject all with explicit message = "不做半途而废的静默降级". Yes: 400 with message.
- `DELETE /api/sessions/:id/revisions` → 200 `{ ok, revisions: [] }`.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/revisions` → same shape as PUT response (for loading a session).
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1` / `resume.doc` / `resume.txt` → 404 if no revisions? Better: allow even with zero revisions (exports original), but the UI only shows it when ≥1. Hmm, exporting the original as "改写后的简历" is misleading. Return 409 if zero revisions: 「还没有采纳任何改写，导出等于原样复印」. Good honest behavior. Actually simpler: allow it — no. 409 is a nice touch and testable.

Let me check what `Suggestion` needs for candidates: `claimId`, `suggestion`. From SessionView.suggestions (SuggestOutcome[]).

Put the change log in the **report** (报告里已有改写建议), and in the resume doc put only a small footer note + optional appendix when `withLog` true. The export is for the user to inspect/save; the PDF is the resume. Compromise: `?log=1` includes the appendix; default off. Default off = clean resume. UI button 「改写记录」? Keep default off, mention in footer that a change log is in the report. Simpler: no appendix. Just footer one line. Good.

Now I have enough context. Let me plan item 8 implementation.

Design for item 8 (需求8: 确认问题后自动改简历并导出 PDF/DOC):

Server side:
1. New shared module? The revision logic (applying suggestions to resume text) is pure — should go in `packages/shared/src/revise.ts`:
   - `ResumeRevision { claimId, before, after, anchor }`? Actually a revision edit = {id, claimId, start, end, before, after, acceptedAt}.
   - `applyEdits(source, edits): {text, applied, skipped}` — sort by start desc, replace; verify source.slice(start,end) === before, else skip (stale edit).
   - `planEdits(source, suggestions)` maybe.

Let me design more concretely:

`packages/shared/src/revise.ts`:
- `export interface ResumeEdit { id: string; claimId: string; start: number; end: number; before: string; after: string; }`
- `export function applyResumeEdits(source: string, edits: readonly ResumeEdit[]): { text: string; applied: ResumeEdit[]; skipped: {edit, reason}[] }`
  - Sort edits by start descending; for each, check `source.slice(start,end) === before` → replace. Since we go descending, offsets of earlier edits stay valid.
  - Overlap detection: if two edits overlap → the later (higher start) applies, the earlier is skipped with reason 'overlap'.
- `export function editFromSuggestion(claimId, suggestion, idSuffix?)`.
- Maybe `export function revisionDiffLines(before, after)` for display? Keep simple: word-level? Skip.

Server:
- schema v5: new table `revision` — records accepted edits per session. Columns: session_id, id (claim id? no — revision id), claim_id, start, end, before_text, after_text, created_at, revoked_at? Actually "撤销" needs ability to remove. Simplest: a revision row per accepted suggestion; delete = revoke (delete row). Keep `revision` table with PK (session_id, id) where id = claimId (one revision per claim makes sense — accepting a different suggestion for same claim replaces). Hmm but multiple claims each one edit. Use claim_id as id → PRIMARY KEY (session_id, claim_id). That's simple and idempotent: accepting again updates.

Table:
```
CREATE TABLE revision (
  session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES session(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  claim_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  start INTEGER NOT NULL,
  end INTEGER NOT NULL,
  before_text TEXT NOT NULL,
  after_text TEXT NOT NULL,
  accepted_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (session_id, claim_id)
)
```

Repo functions: `saveRevision(db, {...})`, `listRevisions(db, sessionId)`, `deleteRevision(db, sessionId, claimId)`, `clearRevisions`.

Pipeline / new module `packages/server/src/revision.ts`:
- `buildRevisionView(session, edits): { resumeText (original), revisedText, edits: [{claimId,before,after,start,end,status}], changedChars, skipped }`
- Also render revised resume to HTML/plain for export: reuse report-html STYLE.

HTTP:
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/revision` → JSON { original, revised, edits, changed }

- for each: check `resumeText.slice(start,end) === before`; if not, try `resolveAnchor`? No — must be exact slice to be safe. Also must check overlapping revisions: two claims could anchor overlapping spans. Then applying both is unsafe. Simpler: iterate sorted desc; if revision.end > nextStart (i.e., overlaps previously applied lower bound), skip with reason「与已采纳的另一条建议区间重叠」.

Let me implement:

```ts
export function applyRevisions(resumeText: string, revisions: readonly Revision[]): ReviseResult {
  const ordered = revisions.slice().sort((a, b) => b.start - a.start || (a.claimId < b.claimId ? -1 : 1));
  const applied: Revision[] = [];
  const skipped = [];
  let text = resumeText;
  let guard = -1; // 已经占用的最左边界
  for (const r of ordered) {
    if (!Number.isInteger(r.start) || !Number.isInteger(r.end) || r.start < 0 || r.end > resumeText.length || r.end <= r.start) {
      skipped.push({claimId: r.claimId, reason: '区间不合法'}); continue;
    }
    if (guard >= 0 && r.end > guard) { skipped.push(...'与另一条已采纳的改写重叠'); continue; }
    const original = resumeText.slice(r.start, r.end);
    if (!isGrounded(...)) ... 
    text = text.slice(0, r.start) + r.after + text.slice(r.end);
    applied.push({...r, before: original});
    guard = r.start;
  }
  return { text, applied, skipped };
}
```

Wait, guard logic: sorted desc, first applied has largest start. Next one's end must be <= previous start (guard). Yes correct.

```ts
/**
 * 采纳改写建议：把「改前 / 改后」真正落到简历文本上。
 * ...
 */
import type { Suggestion } from './domain.ts';
import { isGrounded } from './anchor.ts';
import { shortHash } from './util.ts';

export interface Revision {
  claimId: string;
  start: number;
  end: number;
  before: string;
  after: string;
}

export interface RevisionSkip {
  claimId: string;
  reason: string;
}

export interface ReviseResult {
  text: string;
  applied: Revision[];
  skipped: RevisionSkip[];
}

export function revisionOf(claimId: string, suggestion: Suggestion): Revision {
  return {
    claimId,
    start: suggestion.anchor.start,
    end: suggestion.anchor.end,
    before: suggestion.before,
    after: suggestion.after,
  };
}

export function applyRevisions(resumeText, revisions): ReviseResult
export function reviseDigest(text): string
```

```js
const NL = String.fromCharCode(10);
const body = String.raw`/**
 * 采纳改写建议：把「改前 / 改后」真正落到简历文本上。
 *
 * 三条硬约束，写在代码里而不是写在文档里：
 *
 * 1. **只认原文区间，不认客户端传来的文本。** 采纳请求只带 claimId，改前改后
 *    一律从数据库里那份建议读出来；否则「把简历改一遍」就变成了一个接受任意
 *    字符串替换的接口。
 * 2. **区间必须落在原文上。** 用 isGrounded 复核一次，锚不上的建议一律跳过并
```

```js
const NL = String.fromCharCode(10);
const old = String.raw`export function applyRevisions(resumeText: string, revisions: readonly Revision[]): ReviseResult {
  const ordered = revisions.slice().sort((a, b) => {
    if (b.start !== a.start) return b.start - a.start;
    return a.claimId < b.claimId ? -1 : a.claimId > b.claimId ? 1 : 0;
  });

  const applied: Revision[] = [];
  const skipped: RevisionSkip[] = [];
  let text = resumeText;
  // 已经占用的最左边界：倒序应用时，下一条的 end 不能越过它
  let guard = resumeText.length + 1;

  for (const item of ordered) {
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Let me compress the older ranges now, then continue fixing the last failing test.

The remaining failure: "「改前」展示的永远是原文逐字内容" — sloppy.before = '抗压 能力强' vs actual '抗压能力强'. normalizeWithMap: space is preserved as a single space (' '), not removed! Look at normalizeWithMap: whitespace → pushes ' ' if !prevWasSpace. So '抗压 能力强' normalizes to '抗压 能力强' while actual normalizes to '抗压能力强'. Not equal.

So my test's expectation was wrong-ish, or sameText should ignore whitespace differences. Since the anchor stage uses normalizeWithMap which keeps single spaces, a model quote with an extra space would NOT have anchored either. So actually being strict here is consistent. But the intent of the test — "before 展示的永远是原文逐字内容" — I should change the test to use a punctuation difference instead (full-width vs half-width) which normalization does unify. Let me change the test: actual '抗压能力强' vs claimed '抗压能力强'? No difference. Use the resume line with punctuation: '学习能力强，抗压能力强。' and claimed '学习能力强,抗压能力强.' (half-width) — normalization maps full-width to half-width so they match. That demonstrates the point.

Let me compress first, then fix.

</details>

So: after a session run, the UI shows suggestions per claim. User can accept/check each suggestion; then apply revisions to resume text → new resume text → export as PDF/DOC.

Let me look at the existing structure: assembleSession in pipeline.ts, viewsOfContracts, the web ClaimBoard, and report-html.ts (which already makes printable HTML → PDF via window.print, and .doc via application/msword). That's the pattern to reuse for resume export.

Plan for item 8:

Server:
1. `POST /api/sessions/:id/revise` — body `{ accepted: string[] }` (claim ids) → returns `{ text, applied, skipped, digest, before, after }`? Actually the resume text: session stores resume text? Let me check schema: session table has resume text and jd. Yes likely.
2. `POST /api/sessions/:id/export?format=pdf|doc` or `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.?(pdf|doc)` with accepted ids as query. Better: GET with query `?accepted=C1,C2&format=pdf`.

Hmm, but a GET returning a document with print JS is fine (report.html does `?print=1`).

Design: 
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.md` (or `/revised.md`), `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1`, `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.doc` — with `accepted` query param listing claim ids.

Web:
- In ClaimBoard / a new panel: each suggestion gets a checkbox "采纳" (default on? probably default checked for suggestions with status suggested). Then a bar at bottom: "按采纳的 N 条改写简历 → 预览 / 导出 PDF / 导出 Word".
- Preview: show revised text with highlighted changes (movedSpans).

Also should persist accepted choices? Simplest: client-side state, plus server computes revision from accepted ids. Persisting acceptance in DB would need schema v5. Hmm — is persistence needed? The replay/history feature: reopening a session should restore accepted state. That's nicer but more work. Given the competition emphasis on reproducibility, persisting accepted decisions is valuable: it records the human's decisions. But scope: item 8 says "用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出".

I think persisting is worth it — but let's weigh time. We have items 7, 10, 11, 12 remaining plus video. Persisting acceptance = schema v5 (add table `revision` with session_id, claim_id, accepted, at) + repo + HTTP + tests. That's moderate. I'll do it — it makes the flow reproducible and matches the project's thesis (decisions recorded). Actually, alternative: store in session.meta.revision = {accepted: [...], digest}. meta_json already exists and saveSessionMeta exists. That's much cheaper and fits "整场的一个决策" semantics. But meta is used for panel/model... adding a field is fine.

Hmm, but a cleaner design: a `revision` table keyed by (session_id, claim_id) allows per-claim timestamps. Yet meta is simpler and the whole set is saved atomically. I'll use meta with a dedicated key `revision: {accepted: string[], at: string, digest: string}`.

Now I need to plan item 8: 确认问题后自动改简历，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出.

Let me review the plan. The user requirement #8: "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出".

So the design:
- Frontend: after a session has suggestions, each suggestion card has an "采纳" checkbox. User confirms → the resume text gets modified accordingly (via applyRevisions).
- Provide export of the revised resume as PDF or DOC.

Server side needs:
- Persist accepted revisions per session (meta.revisions? or a table). Let's think: store accepted revision claimIds in session meta, and produce revised text on demand. Since resumeText is stored in session, revised text = applyRevisions(resumeText, revisions) — deterministic, so we only need to store which claimIds are accepted. But revisions are derived from suggestions which are stored in verdict rows (advice_json). So we can reconstruct: accepted claimIds → revisionOf(claimId, suggestion) → applyRevisions.

That's elegant and avoids storing the revised text. But careful: `applied` ordering deterministic. Good.

API:
- `PUT /api/sessions/:id/revisions` body `{accepted: string[]}` → validates claimIds exist and have suggestions; saves to meta.acceptedRevisions; returns `{text, applied, skipped, digest}`.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.md` / `.html` / `.doc` for export. Maybe `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume?format=html|doc|md`.
- Also a highlighted HTML export showing what changed.

Let me think about what's most valuable and simple: 
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html` — printable HTML of revised resume with changes highlighted (using movedSpans), `?print=1` auto print → PDF.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.doc` — same HTML with msword type.

Frontend:
- In ClaimBoard suggestion area, add a checkbox "采纳这条修改".
- A new panel/section "改写后的简历" with live preview and three export buttons (Markdown / PDF / Word), plus "全部采纳".

Server: new module `packages/server/src/resume-export.ts` with renderResumeHtml(revised view) and file names.

Also need to store the revision state? Better: apply is computed on demand from accepted claim ids. Store accepted ids in session meta (`revisions: string[]`) so it's durable — user can come back. POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions {accepted: [...]} saves to meta and returns the revised text + applied + skipped. Then export endpoints read meta.

Now I need to design item 8: "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出".

So: in the web UI, each suggestion (改写建议) should have a "采纳" checkbox/button; user confirms; then app applies revisions to resume text; then exports revised resume as PDF or DOC.

Server side: need endpoint `POST /api/sessions/:id/revise` that takes accepted claim ids (or explicit revisions), applies via applyRevisions, returns { text, applied, skipped, digest }. And `POST /api/sessions/:id/resume.docx` / `.doc` / `.pdf`? PDF: we said server doesn't generate PDF (ADR-0006) — for report. But for resume export, requirement 8 says "提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出". Consistent with existing approach: provide a printable HTML with ?print=1 (user prints to PDF) and .doc for Word. That keeps zero dependencies. Let me do: 
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/revise` → JSON with revised text + applied/skipped (no persistence? or persist to meta). Probably persist nothing — but for export we need the server to know the accepted set. Simpler: the revise endpoint returns text; export endpoints take the accepted ids as query param: `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?claims=C1,C2&print=1` and `.doc`. Hmm, but the text is potentially long; query param of claim ids is short. That works and is stateless.

Actually better: store the revised resume in meta (`meta.revision = {accepted: [...], digest, text}`) so exports are reproducible and history shows it. Let's keep it simpler but honest: 
- POST /api/sessions/:id/revise with body {accepted: string[]} → computes revisions from the session's stored suggestions, applies, saves `meta.revision = { accepted, digest, appliedCount, skipped, text }` via saveSessionMeta, returns { text, applied, skipped, digest, pending }.
- GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html (?print=1) and /resume.doc render the stored revised text (fallback: original resume text) as a clean resume document with applied changes highlighted optionally.

That gives PDF via print, DOC via msword. Good and no deps.

Also need to guard: session must be done? Not necessarily; suggestions exist after run.

Web side: in ClaimBoard suggestion card add a checkbox "采纳这条改写" (data-shot=`adopt-<claimId>`), and a bottom bar in the right panel or a new "改写" panel showing count + 导出 PDF / 导出 Word / 恢复原样 buttons. Need state for accepted ids.
